## Document 012: 115年10月海關進出口貿易初步統計 (2026-11-09)

Extraction status: ok

115 年 10 月海關進出口貿易初步統計 
一、進出口貿易變動 
（一）出口 
10 月出口 967.6 億美元，創歷年單月新高，較上月增 10.9%，較上
年同月增 58.9%(按新臺幣計算增 66.6%)；累 計1 至 10 月出口 7,582.9
億美元，較上年同期增 47.7%(按新臺幣計算增 51.1%)。 
（二）進口 
10 月進口 688.9 億美元，亦創歷年單月新高，較上月增 8.3%，較上
年同月增 75.1%(按新臺幣計算增 83.5%)；累 計1 至 10 月進口 5,697.6
億美元，較上年同期增 44.6%(按新臺幣計算增 47.6%)。 
（三）出入超 
10 月出超 278.8 億美元，為單月新高，較上年同月增 63.3 億美元；
累計 1 至 10 月出超 1,885.4 億美元，較上年同期增 689.7 億美元。

### Page 2

二、出口主要貨品 
（一）與上年同月比較 
受惠於人工智慧(AI)、 高效能運算及雲端服務等應用商機持續拓展，
加上適逢傳統外銷旺季，以及價格攀升效應，10 月出口較上年同月
增 358.7 億美元(+58.9%)，主要變動如下： 
1. 資通與視聽產品出口 476.0 億美元(歷年單月新高)， 較上年同月增
200.7 億美元(+72.9%)，其中電腦及其附屬單元增 151.6 億美元
(+67.5%)、 電腦之零附件增18.5 億美元(+1.0 倍)、 儲存媒體增17.3
億美元(+2.1 倍)、交換器及路由器增 11.7 億美元(+1.3 倍)。 
2. 電子零組件出口 326.8 億美元(歷年單月新高)， 較上年同月增124.3
億美元(+61.4%)，其中積體電路增 120.5 億美元(+63.0%)、印刷電
路增 1.9 億美元(+38.5%)、 電容器及電阻器增1.2 億美元(+48.6%)。 
3. 基本金屬及其製品出口 28.8 億美元，較上年同月增 7.6 億美元
(+35.7%)，其中銅及其製品增 4.7 億美元(+1.2 倍)、鋼鐵及其製品
增 1.9 億美元(+16.4%)。 
4. 機械出口 28.8 億美元(歷年單月新高)，較上年同月增 7.5 億美元
(+35.5%)，其中生產半導體等機械增 2.9 億美元(+57.5%)、機器、
工廠或實驗室設備增 1.4 億美元(+70.0%)。 
5. 礦產品出口 15.5 億美元，較上年同月增 6.5 億美元(+71.6%)，其
中石油煉製品增 5.7 億美元(+72.2%)。

（二）與上年同期比較 
累計 1 至 10 月出口較上年同期增 2,448.3 億美元(+47.7%)，以資通
與視聽產品增 1,423.3 億美元(+72.5%)、電子零組件增 850.5 億美元
(+47.5%)增加較多，礦產品、機械亦各增 41.9 億美元(+36.8%)、41.5
億美元(+19.8%)。

生產等出現結構性轉變，爰另予併計。

三、進口主要貨品 
（一）與上年同月比較 
隨 AI 產業鏈國際分工與出口衍生需求之帶動，積體電路、資通產品
及半導體設備進口持續強勁，加上國際原物料行情居高，10 月總進
口較上年同月增 295.5 億美元(+75.1%)，主要變動如下： 
1. 農工原料進口458.3 億美元， 較上年同月增195.0億美元(+74.0%)，
其中電子零組件增 136.1 億美元(+1.1 倍)、礦產品增 22.8 億美元
(+58.0%)、電腦之零附件增 16.2 億美元(+70.0%)、基本金屬增 6.2
億美元(+33.4%)、塑化原料增 5.0 億美元(+22.0%)。 
2. 資本設備進口 163.4 億美元， 較上年同月增 84.1 億美元(+1.1 倍)，
其中資通設備增 46.9 億美元(+2.0 倍)、半導體設備增 22.4 億美元
(+83.4%)、交通運輸設備增 7.6 億美元(+7.0 倍)。 
3. 消費品進口 61.1 億美元，較上年同月增 18.8 億美元(+44.5%)，其
中固態非揮發性儲存裝置增 16.0 億美元(+5.3 倍)、食品增 0.5 億
美元(+5.1%)、小客車增 0.4 億美元(+10.2%)。

四、主要出口國家/地區 
（一）與上年同月比較 
10 月對美國、歐洲、日本出口創新高 
1. 對美國出口 329.3 億美元，較上年同月增 118.1 億美元(+55.9%)，
其中資通與視聽產品增 98.3 億美元(+56.6%)、電子零組件增 13.5
億美元(+1.7 倍)、機械增 2.4 億美元(+41.6%)。 
2. 對中國大陸與香港出口 204.4 億美元，較上年同月增 61.4 億美元
(+42.9%)，其中電子零組件增 47.4 億美元(+48.0%)、資通與視聽
產品增 5.6 億美元(+36.5%) 、基本金屬及其製品增 3.2 億美元
(+95.2%)。 
3. 對東協出口 184.6 億美元，較上年同月增 84.1 億美元(+83.8%)，
其中電子零組件增 38.6 億美元(+75.5%)、資通與視聽產品增 36.5
億美元(+1.4 倍)、礦產品增 5.0 億美元(+2.4 倍)。 
4. 對歐洲出口 59.5 億美元，較上年同月增 18.7 億美元(+46.0%)，其
中資通與視聽產品增 10.7 億美元(+47.9%)、電子零組件增 3.3 億
美元(+81.3%)、機械增 1.2 億美元(+50.8%)。 
5. 對日本出口 36.7 億美元，較上年同月增 12.2 億美元(+49.6%)，其
中電子零組件增 7.5 億美元(+63.2%)、資通與視聽產品增 3.2 億美
元(+1.3 倍)、化學品增 0.5 億美元(+47.0%)。 
（二）與上年同期比較 
累計 1 至 10 月對美國出口 2,407.1 億美元 ， 較上年同期增891.1 億美
元(+58.8%)，對中國大陸與香港出口 1,805.3 億美元，增 416.6 億美
元(+30.0%)， 對東協出口1,524.1 億美元 ， 增547.6 億美元(+56.1%)，
對歐洲出口 481.3 億美元 ， 增157.1 億美元(+48.5%)， 對日本出口314.4
億美元，增 70.5 億美元(+28.9%)。

五、主要進口國家/地區 
（一）與上年同月比較 
10 月自東協進口 134.1 億美元 ，較上年同月增 82.3 億美元(+1.6 倍)，
自南韓進口 130.0 億 美 元，增73.1 億美元(+1.3 倍)， 自中國大陸與香
港進口 128.3 億美元，增 51.9 億美元(+67.9%)，自日本進口 62.0 億
美元，增 18.8 億美元(+43.4%)，自美國進口 53.6 億美元，增 14.7 億
美元(+37.9%)，自歐洲進口 48.2 億美元，增 9.5 億美元(+24.6%)。 
（二）與上年同期比較 
累計1 至10 月自主要國家/地區進口與上年同期比較 ， 自東協增85.6%、
自南韓增 72.7%、自中國大陸與香港增 45.3%、自日本增 27.4%、自
美國增 23.0%、自歐洲增 12.1%。

六、主要國家/地區貿易概況 
（一）今年 1-9 月主要國家/地區出口均呈上升，其中南韓(1-10 月)、香港、
新加坡受 AI 相關需求帶動科技供應鏈及區域貿易活動熱絡，累計出
口分別年增 60.6%、52.3%、41.3%；中國大陸、美國各年增 21.3%
及 14.3%，德國(1-8 月)、日本各增 8.8%、8.3%。 
（二）主要國家/地區進口同步成長，累計 1-9 月以香港進口年增 51.8%較
高，新加坡、 中國大陸、南 韓(1-10 月)分別年增 39.0%、27.5%及 21.4%，
德國(1-8 月)、日本、美國各增 8.4%、8.2%、6.7%。

### Page 7

7
圖 5 2026 年主要國家/地區出進口年增率
七、綜合分析
（一）隨全球人工智慧(AI)基礎建設投資強勁成長，帶動相關供應鏈需求擴
增，加以外銷產品價格攀升，10 月出口達 967.6 億美元，首度突破
900 億美元，年增 58.9%，連續 36 個月正成長；進口688.9 億美元，
同為歷年單月最高，年增 75.1%，主要受惠於 AI 產業之國際分工與
出口引申需求增加，以及國際原物料行情高漲；出超 278.8 億美元亦
創新高。累計 1 至 10 月出口年增 47.7%、進口增 44.6%。
（二）10 月出口主要貨品中，資通與視聽產品、電子零組件出口值雙創單月
新高，各年增 72.9%及 61.4%，主因 AI、高效能運算及雲端服務等應
用商機熱絡；其餘貨類出口平均增 25.7%，其中礦產品因油價支撐增
71.6%，機械、電機產品受惠於半導體業者擴充產能及AI 需求外溢，
增幅皆達 3 成， 基本金屬及其製品亦增35.7%。累 計1 至 10 月資通與
視聽產品、電子零組件合占總出口比重約 8 成，年增 60.6%。

（三）10 月對主要市場出口年增率均逾 4 成，以對東協增 83.8%較突出，
對美國、日本、歐洲出口規模皆為單月新高，各增 55.9%、49.6%及
46.0%， 對中國大陸與香港增42.9%。累 計1 至 10 月對五大市場出口
增幅介於 28%~59%， 其中對美國出口年增 58.8%、對東協增 56.1%，
占總出口比重各為 31.7%及 20.1%，分別寫下近 36 年同期及歷年同
期新高紀錄。
（四）展望未來，全球經濟仍面臨地緣政治 衝突、美國貿易政策走向等不
確定因素，惟隨人工智慧應用逐漸擴散至推論、代理式 AI，且國際
雲端服務業者持續布建 AI 基礎設施，帶動半導體、伺服器等相關供
應鏈需求延續，加以科技新品相繼推 出、年底消費旺季來臨之挹注，
我國第 4 季出口可望維持強勁成長態勢。

美 
國 
企業活動維持擴張，消費信心與房市疲弱，通膨及關稅風險仍存 
經濟 
⚫ 10 月美國綜合 PMI 產出指數初值由 58.4 降至 57.6，企業活動仍強勁擴
張，內需及出口帶動訂單與就業成長；惟油價、關稅及供應鏈瓶頸推升
成本，售價漲幅創 2022 年 8 月以來新高，通膨壓力持續升溫，後續須
關注聯準會政策動向。 
⚫ 9 月美國住宅市場續顯疲弱，建築許可及新屋開工分別年減 3.0%及
7.4%，反映住宅投資動能放緩。儘管多戶住宅許可與完工數回升，但整
體營建活動仍偏弱，後續仍須關注房貸利率、住房負擔能力及營建成本
對市場復甦的影響。 
⚫ 密西根大學公布之 10 月美國消費者信心指數降至 48.0，月減 0.2%、年
減 10.4%，顯示民眾對目前經濟環境仍缺乏信心，雖然對未來景氣的看
法略有改善，但高物價與借貸成本仍讓消費者感到壓力。此外，未來一
年及長期通膨預期分別升至 4.7%及 3.5%，反映民眾仍擔心物價持續上
漲。 
重要 
事件 
⚫ 10 月 23 日川普宣布調整加拿大商品 50%關稅清單，10 月 30 日生效，
豁免部分紙製品、機械及包裝材料，並新增地毯、油漆等課稅項目。外
媒認為此舉與期中選舉有關，有助降低部分企業成本，但美加貿易摩擦
仍未化解。 
短評 
⚫ 10 月美國綜合產出指數雖略為下滑 ， 但仍維持高檔 ， 顯示內需與就業持
續支撐企業活動擴張。然而，消費者信心仍處低檔，反映高物價與借貸
成本持續壓抑購買意願，儘管民眾對未來景氣的看法略有改善，但新屋
開工及建築許可同步下滑，顯示消費與住宅投資前景仍面臨挑戰。另一
方面，美國調整部分加拿大商品關稅，雖有助減輕部分企業進口成本，
但雙邊貿易摩擦尚未化解，政策不確定性仍存。

國際經濟金融週報 
兆豐國際商業銀行 徵信處 
115.10.27 
一、 美國—企業活動維持擴張，消費信心與房市疲弱，通膨及關稅風險仍存 
標普全球(S&P Global)公布，2026 年 10 月美國綜合 PMI 產出指數(Composite 
PMI Output Index) 初值由上月的 58.4 降至 57.6，其中服務業採購經理人指數
(Services PMI)由 58.8 降至 57.9，製造業採購經理人指數(Manufacturing PMI)則由
55.9 微升至 56.0，創 2022 年 5 月以來新高，企業活動雖略有放緩，但仍維持強
勁擴張。需求方面，內需穩健及出口改善帶動整體新訂單增速創逾 4 年半新高，
商品出口訂單亦為 2025 年 6 月以來首次增加；企業持續擴增人力，整體就業增
速為 2022 年 6 月以來次高。另一方面，未完成訂單增加、供應商交貨延遲加劇，
加上油價上漲及關稅推升能源、運輸與原物料成本，使企業投入成本維持高檔，
帶動售價漲幅升至 2022 年 8 月以來最高。儘管企業對未來一年營運前景的樂觀
程度略降，但仍處於今年相對高檔。整體而言，美國經濟在內外需及就業支撐下
維持擴張，惟供應鏈瓶頸與成本上升加劇通膨壓力，後續仍須關注物價走勢及聯
準會貨幣政策動向。 
密西根大學公布，2026 年 10 月美國消費者信心指數終值降至 48.0，略低於
上月的 48.1，月減 0.2%、年減 10.4%，並較今年 2 月下降約 15%，顯示消費者信
心持續處於低檔。分項來看，當前經濟狀況指數由 50.9 降至 46.7，月減 8.3%、
年減 20.3%；消費者預期指數則由 46.3 回升至 48.8，月增 5.4%、年減 3.0%，反
映民眾對未來景氣的看法略有改善，但整體信心仍偏弱。調查指出，高利率及購
買負擔壓力使耐久財購買條件下降 18%，拖累當前經濟狀況指數；未來一年企業
景氣預期雖改善約 14%，但仍低於去年同期 16%。通膨預期方面，未來一年通膨
預期由 4.6%升至 4.7%， 長期通膨預期亦由3.4%升至 3.5%， 雙雙連續第二個月攀
升，創今年 5 月以來最高水準。密西根大學指出，2025 年關稅政策衝擊及 2026
年伊朗衝突引發的能源價格上漲，使民眾通膨預期再度升溫，且長期預期已明顯
高於 2019 年及 2024 年水準。整體而言，10 月美國消費者信心大致持平，但高物
價與借貸成本仍壓抑購買意願，加上短期及長期通膨預期持續上升，可能增加物
價回穩的難度，後續仍須關注能源價格、家庭消費及通膨預期變化對聯準會貨幣
政策的影響。

### Page 6

用相關內容。

5

2026 年 9 月美國住宅市場建築許可年化總數降至 140.0 萬戶，月減0.2%、年
減 3.0%，其中單戶住宅建築許可降至 84.5 萬戶，月減 4.0%、年減 4.8%，惟 5 戶
以上多戶住宅建築許可增至 50.3 萬戶，月增 5.9%、年增 1.0%，顯示不同住宅類
型開發規劃表現分歧。新屋開工年化數降至 122.1 萬戶，月減5.2%、年減 7.4%，
其中單戶住宅開工 85.9 萬戶，較去年同期仍增 2.6%，但多戶住宅開工降至 34.9
萬戶，年減25.9%，拖累整體營建動能。施工方面，在建住宅維持128.0 萬戶，與
上月持平但較去年減少 3.3%；完工年化數則回升至 119.2 萬戶，月增2.2%，主要
受多戶住宅完工月增 14.9%帶動，惟整體仍較去年同期減少 14.0%。整體而言，
美國住宅市場呈現建築許可及新屋開工下滑、在建規模持平及完工略有回升的態
勢，反映住宅營建活動仍偏弱，後續仍須關注房貸利率走勢、住房負擔能力及營
建成本變化對住宅市場復甦的影響。 
美國總統川普 10 月 23 日依據《1930 年關稅法》第 338 條簽署公告，調整部
分加拿大商品加徵 50%關稅的適用範圍，並自 10 月 30 日起生效。此次調整取消
膠合板、部分紙製品、機械與冷凍設備、包裝用木箱及紙箱、新鮮與乾燥海藻等
商品的額外關稅，同時新增閥門、地毯、油漆、洗髮精、可可粉、木製棺材、空
調設備及獨木舟等課稅項目，呈現局部鬆綁與新增課稅並行的政策方向。白宮表
示 ， 此舉旨在減輕加拿大貿易措施對美國企業造成的負擔 ， 並維護國內產業利益 。
由於緬因州農業及製造業高度依賴加拿大進口物資，共和黨參議員柯林斯 (Susan 
Collins)積極向白宮爭取關稅豁免 ， 外媒亦將此次調整與11 月 3 日期中選舉連結 。
整體而言，新措施有助降低部分美國企業的進口成本，但美加貿易摩擦尚未完全
化解，後續仍須關注雙邊談判及關稅政策對供應鏈的影響。 
二、歐元區—法國動盪短期內難降溫，料將使央行保持審慎觀望態度 
 歐元區 10 月綜合採購經理人指數(PMI)下降 1.3 點至 51.9，抵銷上月的強勁
增幅，但為連續五個月高於 50 的榮枯線，主要係受到法國大規模抗議活動的影
響，使法國綜合 PMI 下降 2.7 點至 48.4；此外，德國綜合PMI 下降 1 點至 52.8，
亦反映近期通膨壓力增加 ， 使民眾傾向保守消費 。 若以產業區分 ， 歐元區服務業 、
製造業 PMI 分別降 1.3、0.5 點至 51.7、52.9；惟德國製造業 PMI 上升至相當強勁
的 54.2，反映德國政府擴張軍備投資以及基礎建設之需求，持續推動製造業生產
動能。

### Page 7

本報告係根據研究資料及各項可靠數據作成，兆豐銀行保留報告內容所有權利，未經本行許可，請勿引
用相關內容。

6

法國抗爭活動規模逐漸擴大 ， 各大工會與抗議團體已宣布在 10 月 31 日再度
發起大規模遊行，並預告於 11 月 5 日舉行全國性大罷工，抗議主要訴求包括改
善公共服務品質及凍結物價上漲。然而，在財政壓力巨大且國會缺乏穩定執政多
數的情況下，法國政府既無法強力通過重大的財政改革，也難以快速推出安撫反
對派的妥協法案或者公共支出。故部分較為悲觀的機構認為此波動盪可能會延續
至 2027 年 4 月的法國總統大選。受法國局勢影響，近期歐元區各國殖利率普遍
呈現上升趨勢，並對景氣產生暫時性的抑制效果。在此背景下，市場預期歐洲央
行在 10 月 28 日的政策會議將採取觀望立場，後續關注歐洲央行是否將透過政策
訊號或流動性工具，為平息法國主權債務利率而採取行動。 
三、日本—貿易逆差及通膨壓力延續，內需仍具韌性 
 9 月出口雖維持強勁 ， 受半導體製造設備及汽車出口增加所帶動 ， 出口額年增
16.7%；惟進口增幅更為顯著，9 月進口額年增 18.9%，其中原油及液化天然氣進
口額分別大增 51.1%及 39.2%，主要反映中東局勢推升能源價格，以及日本尋求
替代能源供應來源的影響。由於進口額高於出口額，9 月貿易逆差達 5,365 億日
圓，為連續第 5 個月呈現逆差；累計 2026 上半財年（4 至 9 月）貿易逆差亦較上
年同期擴大 98.8%至 2.82 兆日圓。整體而言，出口並未明顯轉弱，近期貿易逆差
擴大主要反映能源進口成本攀升，使進口增幅持續高於出口。 
 9 月全國消費者物價指數(CPI)年增 2.1%，即使政府透過能源及教育補貼等措
施抑制物價，通膨仍高於2%，顯示實際通膨動能更為強勁。從物價結構來看，石
油、原物料及工資成本上升已逐步傳導至終端價格，漲價範圍由家具、醫療用品
及休閒用品等商品 ， 進一步擴散至房屋維護 、 公共運輸 、 住宿及外食等服務項目 ，
顯示企業成本轉嫁範圍逐步擴大，通膨壓力亦由商品向服務領域擴散。隨通膨持
續高於 2%且漲價範圍擴大 ， 亦為日本央行進一步推動貨幣政策正常化提供支撐 。 
 10 月綜合 PMI 持平於 52.3，顯示經濟仍維持穩定擴張，其中製造業 PMI 下
降 0.8 點至 53.3，產出指數亦降至 3 月以來最低，主要反映新訂單及出口訂單成
長放緩，加上稀土金屬等原料供應受限，對生產形成一定壓力，惟生產與訂單仍
處擴張區間，尚未顯示製造業景氣明顯轉弱；服務業 PMI 回升 0.7 點至 52.0，新
業務及出口訂單同步改善，顯示服務需求仍具韌性，為支撐景氣的重要動能。另
一方面，能源、薪資及原物料成本居高不下，使投入價格壓力持續偏高，並逐漸

影響企業對未來景氣的信心。 
四、中國—第三季經濟成長率回升，但前景仍較為不利 
2026 年第三季 GDP 年增率初估值為 4.5%，較第二季的 4.3%上揚 0.2 個百分
點；累計 1-9 月 GDP 年增率則為 4.6%。第三季的二、三級產業年增率分別較第
二季上揚 0.4、0.1 個百分點至 3.4%， 其中製造業 、 批發零售業年增率分別達5.9%、
2.3%為主要動能 ， 抵銷建築業年減4.4%等拖累因素影響 。 整體而言 ， 第三季GDP
年增率上揚有部分受到中秋國慶連假落在 9 月末段(去年同期則於 10 月 1 日開
始)，致基期效果發揮部分影響，相關消費動能仍可能會在 10 月出現暫時性的回
落。 
在需求指標方面，(1)1-9 月累計零售額年增率為 1.1%，持平於 1-8 月的累計
年增率。其中 9 月零售額年增率增加 0.7 個百分點至 1.1%，部分受到新手機上市
帶動買氣，9 月通訊器材消費年增率高達 31.1%，加上中秋國慶長假帶動食品消
費年增率增加 1 個百分點至 2.1%所致 ；(2)1-9 月固定資產投資年減幅維持於 7.2%，
反映供應鏈移轉、政府主導去化過剩產能等因素導致民間投資需求急遽萎縮；萎
縮產業包含化工、醫藥、專用設備、汽車製造業、金屬製品業等；(3)1-9 月房地
產開發投資額年減幅由 19.9%擴大至 20.6%、施工面積年減幅為 12.8%，新建商
品房銷售面積年減幅擴大為 13.2%，房市投資、買氣皆持續低迷。 
受到出口維持高度成長帶動，9 月工業生產年增率較 8 月增加 0.3 個百分點
至 5.5%(累計 1-9 月工業生產年增率持平 1-8 月水準為 5.3%)。依產業別來看，9
月主要受到煤礦開採、化工、化纖等產業擴張影響，主因中秋與國慶長假前發電
業充實庫存水位，促使煤炭企業加快復產，加上油價上漲帶動相關中下游供應鏈
增產；惟高科技產品包含 IC、機器人、新能源車等年增率皆有放慢，反映去年中
秋國慶連假皆落在 10 月，使得去年 9 月工作日較多，致基期相對墊高的影響。 
展望未來 ， 儘管政府10 月初宣布釋放 5,500 億元人民幣的地方政府債務結存
限額，但額度下達到資金撥付往往需要數月時間，預期對於第四季的經濟影響有
限，使今年全年經濟成長率預估將落在 4.5%附近。此外，受歐盟右派勢力崛起及
保護主義影響，歐盟對中國貿易政策可能持續趨嚴，後續仍須關注雙方談判及溝
通結果，未來若外需動能減弱，預期中國政府將持續透過發展特定高科技產業及
扶植內需來支持經濟成長動能，以減輕房市不振及貿易環境惡化的衝擊。

五、印度及東南亞—新、馬出口保持強勁增速，菲律賓央行大舉升息 
(1)印度: 10 月 HSBC 綜合採購經理人指數（PMI）初值上升 0.8 點至 56.7，其中，
製造業 PMI 略下滑 0.2 點至 54.9、服務業 PMI 則上揚 1.0 點至 56.2，皆保持穩健
擴張態勢。由於美國、英國、中東及孟加拉等地的訂單增加，帶動新訂單及出口
訂單加速成長，製造商採購庫存增速創八個月來最快、成品庫存增速亦創歷史新
高，然而聘僱指數降至今年以來最低、未完成訂單下降，反映現階段人力充足。
另一方面，投入成本漲幅連續第七個月下降並降至近一年低點，產品售價漲幅亦
明顯放緩，通膨壓力緩解，有助提升企業獲利能力及消費者購買力，並且給予印
度央行（RBI）貨幣政策空間。 
(2)新加坡：9 月非石油國內出口（NODX）年增 68.4%，增速史上最快，連續第
23 個月正成長，其中，人工智慧需求強勁，帶動電子產品出口躍增 107.6%；非電
子產品出口亦大幅成長 54.2%，主要由船舶和遊艇組件所帶動。按出口目的地劃
分 ， 對香港出口激增 121.1%、 對台灣 、 韓國出口增幅達96%， 對美出口成長 73.2%，
但對印度出口下降 5.6%。展望未來，新加坡金融管理局在 10 月的宏觀經濟評估
報告中表示，直至 2027 年人工智慧 (AI) 驅動的成長將保持相對穩定， 支持未
來幾季經濟成長表現。由於基數較高以及人工智慧相關投資預計放緩，全球利率
上升可能會對新加坡主要貿易夥伴的消費和非人工智慧投資造成壓力，可能會影
響外部需求，薪資成長放緩、招聘謹慎以及運輸成本上升可能會在 2026 年下半
年對可自由支配支出和零售活動構成壓力，預計 2027 年 GDP 成長將放緩。 
(3)印尼：印尼央行 （BI）10 月會議連續第四次會議維持基準利率(7 天期逆回購利
率,BI-Rate)於 5.75%不變，貸款便利利率（LFR）維持在 6.50%不變，但將隔夜存
款便利利率（DFR）調高 25 個基點至 5%，旨在提高印尼盾資產的回報率，藉此
吸引並留住外資，同時宣布分兩階段調降存款準備率，11 月 1 日起下調 1 個百分
點至 8%，12 月 1 日起進一步降至 7%，以提供銀行體系流動性。整體而言，印尼
央行爲捍衛印尼盾，在 5 至 6 月間共升息 100 個基點，同時調降外資避險成本，
並採取「資金停留愈久、優惠愈高」的機制吸引外資流入。惟近期國際指數編纂
公司 MSCI 擬將印尼股市由”新興市場”降級為”獨立市場”，引發資金外流疑慮，
央行選擇利率按兵不動，搭配降準提供銀行體系流動性，以兼顧印尼盾穩定與國
內經濟需求，未來 MSCI 檢討進展(最快 2027 年 4 月公布)及印尼盾走勢，將持續
牽動央行後續政策。

源擴散至整體經濟。BSP 亦擔憂中東情勢推升國際油價、運輸費率調漲，以及聖

(5)馬來西亞：9 月出口額年增 54.5%至 MYR2,151 億元，其中電子&電氣產品出
口大增 69.2%，持續反映 AI 帶動的強勁需求，礦產品出口亦大幅成長 56.3%，抵
銷農產品出口續跌 5.7%的影響，對台灣、美國出口大增 175.2%、89.9%、對新加
坡出口增幅亦達 50.4%。9 月進口額成長 52.7%至 MYR1,814 億元，受惠出口引
申需求，中間產品、資本貨物進口分別成長69.6%、20.9%，消費品進口轉為正成
長 18.2%，自中國和新加坡進口分別成長 67.6%、90.7%。進出口相抵，9 月貿易
順差達 MYR337 億元、年增 64.9%。 
六、台灣—外銷訂單創新高，產業景氣冷熱不均 
9 月外銷訂單達 1,142.1 億美元，再創單月新高，年增 62.4%，主要受惠於人
工智慧 、 高效能運算與雲端服務等應用蓬勃發展 ，以及消費性電子新品陸續推出 ，
帶動相關供應鏈維持高檔；加上國際原物料價格上漲，推升整體接單表現。惟各
產業接單動能仍呈分化，科技產品需求強勁，傳統產業景氣則相對疲弱。經濟部
指出，雖有年底節慶補貨潮挹注，但傳統產業部分產能過剩，終端需求仍未見明
顯回升。

## Document 070: 2026年10月物价数据点评：10月物价走势分化，PPI同比创近52个月新高

### Page 1

东方金诚宏观研究

1 
10月物价走势分化，PPI同比创近 52个月新高 
——2026年 10月物价数据点评 
研究发展部执行总监 冯琳 
事件：根据国家统计局11月9日公布的数据，2026年10月，CPI同比上涨0.7%，上月为上涨0.9%，
1-10月CPI累计同比为0.9%，2025年CPI累计同比为0.0%；10月PPI同比上涨5.7%，上月为上涨5.0%，
1-10月累计同比上涨2.7%，2025年累计同比下降2.6%。 
基本观点：10月 CPI同比涨幅收窄，主要原因是蔬菜价格大幅下跌，带动食品价格同比降幅显著扩大，
充分抵消了当月国际油价上涨推动国内汽油价格上调带来的影响。另外，当月国际金价大幅下跌，汽车、
家电等耐用消费品价格继续走低，对整体CPI同比的下拉作用加大。不过，受全球AI投资热潮带动芯片价
格大幅上冲影响，10月国内电脑、手机等电子产品价格继续上涨，对整体物价水平有一定推升作用。10月
剔除变动较大的能源和食品价格、更能反映基本物价水平的核心CPI同比为 0.8%，继续处于偏低水平，背
后主要是当前消费市场仍然呈现明显的供强需弱特征。这也意味着后期促消费政策空间较大。10月 PPI环
比涨幅回落，主要源于当月国际原油、有色金属以及国内煤炭等大宗商品价格在前期较快上涨、绝对价格
水平已偏高的背景下，环比上行幅度明显放缓。不过，在环比延续上涨带动下，10月 PPI同比涨幅进一步
扩大。往后看，11月PPI环比可能转跌，同比涨幅将有所回落，但年底前仍将处于4.0%以上的较快增长区
间。 
具体来看： 
一、10月CPI同比涨幅收窄，主要原因是蔬菜价格大幅下跌，带动食品价格同比降幅显著扩大，充分
抵消了当月国际油价上涨推动国内汽油价格上调带来的影响。另外，当月国际金价大幅下跌，带动国内金
饰品价格同比涨幅急剧收窄，也对整体CPI走势有一定影响；伴随促消费政策效应有所减弱，10月汽车、
家电等耐用消费品价格继续走低，对整体 CPI同比的下拉作用扩大。不过，受全球 AI投资热潮带动芯片
价格大幅上冲影响，10月国内电脑、手机等电子产品价格继续上涨，对整体物价水平有一定推升作用。10
月剔除变动较大的能源和食品价格、更能反映基本物价水平的核心CPI同比为0.8%，继续处于偏低水平，
背后主要是当前消费市场仍然呈现明显的供强需弱特征。这也意味着后期促消费政策空间较大。 
10月CPI环比由升转平，基本符合季节性规律，同比涨幅为0.7%，较上月收窄0.2个百分点。背后的

### Page 2

东方金诚宏观研究

2 
主要原因是当月蔬菜价格大幅下跌，带动食品价格同比降幅显著扩大，充分抵消了当月国际油价上涨推动
国内汽油价格上调带来的影响。数据显示，主要受前期菜价偏高推动供应量大幅上升影响，10月蔬菜价格
环比下降 5.8%，叠加上年同期价格基数偏高，当月蔬菜价格同比下降16.9%，降幅比上月扩大 8.8个百分
点，叠加供应增加导致鲜果价格同比降幅扩大，鸡蛋价格同比高位下行，共同带动当月食品 CPI同比下降
2.9%，降幅较上月扩大 1.2个百分点，对整体 CPI同比的下拉作用达到 0.5个百分点，下拉幅度较上月扩
大0.2个百分点。10月国际原油价格上涨，国内汽油价格上调，对整体CPI同比涨幅的上拉影响达到0.63
个百分点，比上月扩大0.16个百分点，但被食品价格走低充分抵消。 
其它方面，9月美联储加息，10月国际金价下跌 7.2%，叠加上年同期金价基数抬高，带动 10月国内
金饰品价格同比涨幅从上月的 22.5%急剧收窄至 4.5%，下拉当月整体 CPI同比近 0.1个百分点。最后，当
前以旧换新政策对汽车、家电等耐用消费品价格支撑作用减弱，10月这两类补贴类耐用消费品价格环比延
续下行，叠加上年同期基数走高，价格同比分别下降1.9%和2.2%，降幅较上月均有所扩大，也是制约当前
CPI同比涨幅的一个原因。 
不过，受全球 AI投资热潮带动芯片价格上涨影响，10月国内手机、计算机、平板电脑、数据存储设
备等电子产品价格继续上涨，价格同比涨幅总体扩大，对整体CPI同比的推升作用达到0.26个百分点，较
上月扩大0.03个百分点。 
    服务价格方面，10月长假居民旅游出行消费需求集中释放，当月宾馆住宿、飞机票和旅行社收费价格
季节性上涨，但受中秋错期（今年中秋节落在9月，去年落在10月）等影响，当月服务价格同比涨幅较上
月收窄0.1个百分点至0.7%。10月剔除波动较大的能源、食品价格，更能体现基本物价水平的核心CPI同
比为 0.8%，较上月下行 0.2个百分点，继续处于低位，主要受国内消费需求不振、市场供强需弱拖累。这
一方面意味着近期PPI高增难以向下游消费终端传导，另一方面也意味着后期促消费政策空间较大。 
往后看，11月初国际油价大幅下行，受10月全球AI估值大幅调整影响，11月国内电子产品价格涨幅
趋缓，叠加当月食品价格还有下行趋势，我们预计 11月 CPI环比会由平转降，同比涨幅会进一步收窄至
0.6%。整体上看，年底前物价水平都会处在温和偏弱状态。1-10月CPI累计同比为0.9%，低于年初确定的
“2.0%左右”的控制目标，与美、欧、日等经济体通胀形势严峻形成鲜明对照，是当前国内外宏观经济最
大的差异点。这决定了后期国内货币政策将继续坚持“以我为主”，延续适度宽松的主基调。 
二、10月PPI环比涨幅回落，主要源于当月国际原油、有色金属以及国内煤炭等大宗商品价格在前期
较快上涨、绝对价格水平已偏高的背景下，环比上行幅度明显放缓。不过，在环比延续上涨带动下，10月

### Page 3

东方金诚宏观研究

3 
PPI 同比涨幅进一步扩大。往后看，11月 PPI 环比可能转跌，同比涨幅将有所回落，但年底前仍将处于
4.0%以上的较快增长区间。 
10月PPI环比上涨0.7%，涨幅较上月收窄0.5个百分点，但在环比延续上涨带动下，当月PPI同比涨
幅扩大至5.7%，为2022年下半年以来最高水平。 
10月 PPI环比延续上涨但涨幅回落，主要源于当月国际原油、有色金属及国内煤炭等大宗商品价格虽
保持高位，但在前期较快上涨、价格绝对水平已偏高背景下，环比上行幅度明显放缓。其中，10月中东地
缘局势仍未明朗，国际油价高位上行，布油现货价月均值升至 125美元/桶，环比上涨 6.8%，涨幅较上月
大幅回落 21.8个百分点；受迎峰度冬关键准备期电煤需求释放、国内煤炭复产有待进一步推进影响，10
月煤炭价格延续偏强态势，动力煤价格突破 1000元/吨关口，但从月均值看环比涨势有所放缓。在此背景
下，10月石油和天然气开采、煤炭开采等行业价格环比分别上涨6.0%和2.7%，涨幅较上月分别下滑4.4和
6.8个百分点。同时，10月有色金属矿采选业价格环比涨幅回落至0.3%（前值1.6%），有色金属冶炼及压
延加工业价格环比从上月的上涨 1.5%转为下跌 0.1%。此外，在全球 AI投资热潮推动下，年初以来包括芯
片、电脑、手机等电子元器件及电子产品价格快速上涨，成为推动整体PPI走高的重要因素，10月这一势
头仍在延续，但边际动力有所放缓，当月计算机、通信和其他电子设备制造业价格环比涨幅较上月回落
0.3个百分点至0.1%。 
不过，随着能源价格上涨继续向下传导，10月化学原料及化学制品制造、化学纤维制造价格环比分别
上涨 3.1%和 2.4%，涨幅较上月加快 0.7和 0.3个百分点。同时，受益于产能有序调整、供求关系有所改
善，10月非金属矿物制品业价格上涨 0.8%（前值 0.3%），汽车制造业价格在连续 3个月下跌后转为持平
（前值-0.2%）。这些行业价格的积极变化对当月PPI环比涨势形成支撑。 
分大类看，10月生产资料环比上涨0.9%，涨幅较上月放缓0.5个百分点。分项中，采掘工业价格上涨
2.8%（前值 7.7%），原材料工业价格上涨2.0%（前值 2.6%），加工工业价格上涨0.2%（前值 0.2%），显
示上游涨价势头明显回落，中游受成本推动、产能调整等因素影响价格涨幅回落幅度较小，下游涨价动力
依然不足。同比来看，10月 PPI同比扩大至 5.7%，创近 52个月以来新高。其中，10月生产资料价格同比
涨幅较上月加快 0.9个百分点至 7.4%，分项中，采掘工业、原材料工业、加工工业价格分别上涨27.9%、
11.6%和3.5%，涨幅较上月分别加快2.5、2.2和0.1个百分点。从主要影响因素上看，10月国际原油价格
上涨，影响 PPI整体上行 2.2个百分点，影响幅度较上月扩大0.6个百分点，是当月 PPI同比涨幅扩大的
主要原因。

### Page 4

东方金诚宏观研究

4 
生活资料方面，10月价格环比下跌 0.1%，上月为上涨 0.1%，表现依然偏弱，背后是终端需求持续不
足。当月各分项价格环比涨势均有所转弱，其中食品价格持平（前值 0.2%），一般日用品、衣着、耐用消
费品价格环比分别下跌0.1%、0.2%和0.1%。值得一提的是，耐用消费品价格在此前两个月上涨后转跌，显
示半导体等原材料涨价对手机等电子产品价格的推升作用减弱，同时汽车价格下跌对耐用消费品价格形成
一定拖累。同比来看，10月生活资料价格同比下跌0.1%，跌幅与上月持平。分项中，食品价格同比跌幅有
所收窄，衣着和一般日用品价格同比跌幅小幅走阔，耐用消费品价格同比涨幅放缓至1.4%（前值1.6%）。 
往后看，考虑到接下来宏观政策将继续向稳增长方向发力，投资增速有望加快，将对国内主导的钢铁、
水泥等上游原材料价格形成一定拉动。不过，接下来原油、煤炭、有色金属以及芯片等电子元器件及电子
产品价格上行势头料将减弱，其中 11月以来原油价格已出现明显回落。综合来看，预计11月 PPI环比可
能会转跌，同比涨幅将回落至5.1%左右，年底前仍将处于4.0%以上的较快增长水平。

客戶缺料及平台轉換影響拉貨，4Q26 EPS 預估季減 22% 
川湖 3Q26 營收 173.38 億元，季增 60.1%、年增 296.9%，低於預期 7.3%。
7–8 月稼動率 80%，9 月降至 60%，主因客戶記憶體缺料、新舊平台轉換及
新創客戶一次性建置完成，因此維持 3Q26 毛利率預估 87.2%，EPS 下修
7.2%至 119.52 元。4Q26 營收則以 9 月作為基準，客戶拉貨取決於急缺零
組件到位情況，備料完成隨時啟動拉貨。預估 4Q26 營收 135.61 億元，季
減 21.8%，毛利率 86.4%，EPS 下修 28.2%至 92.91 元，季減 22.3%，反
映客戶備料及平台轉換影響。 
1H27 VR 量產與周邊機櫃導入，台美擴產支應成長 
1H27 進入 VR 量產週期，除運算機櫃外，電源、CPU 等周邊 Side Rack 同
步導入，擴大導軌應用並提高單一系統供貨價值。新平台設計與加工難度提
升，帶動高階導軌量價成長。擴產方面，美國休士頓廠已出貨，川湖二廠仍
在興建，預計 2H27 投產；三、五期目前處於設計階段，預計 2027 年動工。
三期規模相當於現有廠房面積，五期與三期相同，擴建完成後整體產能至少
為現有 3 倍。因規模較大，建廠需 2–2.5 年，預計 2029 年底至 2030 年完
工。本中心預估 2027 年營收 730.53 億元，年增 54.8%，EPS 500.57 元。 
維持川湖買進評等，目標價 15,000 元 
考量記憶體缺料、新舊平台轉換及新創客戶一次性建置完成， 本次下修
2026／2027 年營收預估 12.3%／13.7%至 471.78／730.53 億元，年增
169.6%／54.8%；EPS 下修 12.4%／14.0%至 323.39／500.57 元，年增
213.3%／54.8%。看好川湖導軌製造能力， 1H27 VR 量產及周邊機櫃導入
帶動需求，二廠 2H27 投產增加產能，三、五期支應中長期成長。維持
2027 年 30 倍本益比，以 EPS 500.57 元推得目標價 15,000 元，維持買進。 
元大觀點 
⧫  
記憶體缺料、平台轉換及新創客戶一次性建置完成，影響短期拉貨，
下修 3Q26／4Q26 EPS 7%／28%至 119.52／92.91 元。 
⧫  
1H27 VR 量產與周邊機櫃導入帶動高階導軌需求，川湖二廠 2H27 投
產增加供應能力，預估 2027 營收 730.53 億元，年增 54.8%。 
⧫  
下修 2026／2027 年 EPS 預估 12%／14%至 323.39 元／500.57 元，
維持 2027 年 30 倍本益比，目標價調整至 15,000 元，維持買進。

營運分析
3Q26 營收低於預期 7%，EPS 預估下修至 119.52 元
川湖 3Q26 營收 173.38 億元，季增 60.1%、年增 296.9%，低於本中心預估 7.3%。7–8 月稼動率
約 80%，9 月降至 60%，主要受到 1）客戶記憶體缺料，影響整機備料及組裝進度；2）新舊平台
轉換；3）部分 AI 新創客戶一次性建置完成等因素影響，導致 9 月營收月減約 30%。本中心維持
3Q26 毛利率預估 87.2%，季減 0.2 個百分點；營業利益預估下修 7.3%至 141.79 億元，季增
59.5%、年增 376.6%；稅後淨利預估 113.90 億元，季增 60.7%、年增 256.3%，EPS 預估下修
7.2%至 119.52 元。

客戶備料進度影響拉貨節奏，4Q26 營收預估季減 22%
公司表示，客戶拉貨節奏相當不規律，主要取決於急缺零組件取得情況；當記憶體等材料到位、整
機具備組裝條件後，即可啟動拉貨，因此單月出貨仍將隨客戶備料進度波動。目前客戶專案需求預
測未見明顯異常，但缺料與新舊平台轉換使需求釋出時點較難平均分布。
本中心下修 4Q26 營收預估 27.9%至 135.61 億元，季減 21.8%、年增 174.0%；考量出貨規模及
產能利用率調整，毛利率預估下修 0.8 個百分點至 86.4%，季減 0.8 個百分點。營業利益預估
109.79 億元，季減 22.6%、年增 220.6%；稅後淨利預估 88.54 億元，季減 22.3%、年增 152.0%，

EPS 預估下修 22.3%至 92.91 元。

1H27 進入 VR 量產週期，周邊 Side Rack 擴大導軌需求
1H27 進入 Vera Rubin 量產週期，除運算機櫃外，電源、CPU 等周邊 Side Rack 亦須配合整體系
統導入，導軌需求由運算設備延伸至周邊基礎設施。成長來源除新平台出貨增加，亦包含不同功能
機櫃增加，以及導軌設計與加工難度提高，推升單一系統的供貨價值。

各類導軌依機構設計與加工要求定價，部分電源及 CPU 應用的導軌複雜度高於 GPU 應用，產品價
值不隨設備售價等比例下降。川湖既有產品原則上於生命週期內維持議定價格，新世代產品則依升
級規格重新報價，因此平均單價提升主要來自新產品導入及產品組合優化。本中心看好 VR 量產、
ASIC 平台升級及周邊應用增加，帶動 2027 年高階導軌量價成長，預估全年營收 730.53 億元，年
增 54.8%，毛利率 86.5%，年增 0.6 個百分點，EPS 500.57 元。
美國休士頓廠已於 10 月投產；川湖二廠則預計 2H27 投產
川湖二廠目前仍在興建，預計 2H27 投產；美國休士頓廠則已於 9–10 月向首批客戶出貨，完整產
能開出後將增加整體產能約 20%，目前生產效率約為台灣廠的一半，公司透過人員訓練及製程改
善提高效率，2027 年持續提升產出。部分 AI 專案交期急迫，空運成本甚至高於導軌本身，北美在
地生產可縮短交付時間，支援客戶急單及補貨需求。休士頓廠產出提升與二廠 2H27 投產，將分階

段增加供應能力，配合 VR 及其他新平台的出貨需求。
川益三、五期預計 2027 年動工，2029 年底至 2030 年完工
川益三、五期已開始設計，後續須完成審圖及發包，預計 2027 年動工。三期規模相當於目前所有
廠房面積的加總，五期則與三期規模相同；依公司說明，擴建完成後整體產能至少達現有的 3 倍。
由於工程規模較大，建廠時間約需 2–2.5 年，預計 2029 年底至 2030 年完工，支應後續新世代平
台及周邊應用需求。
公司已通過三、五期廠房新建及設備採購預算 197.12 億元，依工程與設備採購進度分期投入，並
規劃發行面額上限合計 100 億元、年期 5 年、票面利率 0%的可轉債，資金用於轉投資川益科技。
三、五期定位為中長期產能布局，2027 年新增供應主要來自休士頓廠產出提升及二廠投產。

### Page 4

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：組裝及零組件產業 2026 年 11 月 4 日 King Slide 第4 頁，共 20頁  
產品結構分析  
預估川湖 2027 年 AI 伺服器營收比重達 84%，多元平台架構支撐長期展望 
川湖具備高承載、高耐用度及客製化滑軌設計能力，AI 伺服器對於滑軌承重、抗震與散熱協同設計
要求提升，形成新進者難以突破的技術門檻。川湖目前 2Q26 產品組合為伺服器 94%、6%，本中
心預估川湖 2026/2027 年 AI 伺服器營收比重分別達 78%/84%，其中以 GB/VR/ASIC 系列為主軸，
並擴產至 NVDIA Groq、Spectrum-X 交換器、AMD MI450 以及 Neo Cloud 等新平台架構。 
展望 2026-2027 年，我們預估川湖於整體伺服器導軌供貨份額達 90%以上，並看好川湖伺服器導
軌成長動能無虞，主因包含 1) GB300 單一機櫃導軌產值估較 GB200 成長 80%，數量由 2.5 萬櫃
提升至 6.8 萬櫃；2) Vera Rubin(VR200) 平台預計 2H26 登場，預計 4Q26 量產，預估 2027 年達
7-8 萬櫃；3) AWS T3 需求強勁，預期 AWS ASIC 晶片量將 2026/2027 年分別年增 25%/70%至
200/340 萬顆；4) 受惠於 AI 訓練與推論需求爆發，預估 2026 年通用型伺服器出貨量將年增 23%。

### Page 5

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：組裝及零組件產業 2026 年 11 月 4 日 King Slide 第5 頁，共 20頁  
同業比較分析  
川湖為 AI 導軌龍頭廠商，專利護城河將確保公司穩居領先地位 
川湖在歐美、台灣及中國皆有完整的專利佈局，目前專利核准數高達 3,641 件，深厚的專利壁壘使
競爭對手極難繞過，無法輕易生產高可靠度產品。同時，川湖具備高承載、高耐用度及客製化滑軌
設計能力，能完美對應 AI 伺服器對滑軌承重、抗震與散熱協同設計的嚴格要求，形成新進者難以
突破的技術門檻。在市場定位與市佔表現方面，對比國內同業南俊國際（ 6584 TT ）在 GB 及 
ASIC 等 AI 導軌出貨比重仍低，川湖的整體伺服器導軌市佔率預估高達 90% 以上，專利護城河將
確保公司穩居領先地位；就獲利率而言，由於川湖的  AI 導軌營收佔比較高，整體獲利能力亦優於
南俊國際。 
我們持續看好公司憑藉極高的技術門檻與產品可靠度，產品線全面涵蓋次世代  AI 伺服器（如  
GB300、Vera Rubin 平台）、ASIC 伺服器、網路交換器及 Neo Cloud 等多元平台，以因應強勁
需求。此外，川湖的擴廠計畫包含：1）美國德州新廠已於 2026 年 10 月開始出貨；2）台灣二廠
預估於 2H27；3）川益三期、五期預期 2029 年底至 2030 年完工，預期將支撐中長期成長。

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：組裝及零組件產業 2026 年 11 月 4 日 King Slide 第7 頁，共 20頁  
財務分析  
川湖整體財務槓桿維持極低且健全的水準，負債比長年保持在  20% 以下，顯著低於同業平均，展
現強健的抗風險能力；流動比率則維持在 5.5 至 8 倍，優於同業平均之表現，顯示公司流動性充裕、
短期償債無虞。在存貨管理方面，存貨周轉天數近年約落在  100 至 150 天區間震盪，略高於同業
平均。受惠於產品組合優化與高毛利訂單放量，ROE 與 ROA 呈現逐年走揚態勢，營運效率持續改
善。整體而言，本中心認為川湖在極佳的資本結構與流動性保護下，獲利能力與資產運用效率同步
提升，財務體質維持高度穩健。

### Page 8

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：組裝及零組件產業 2026 年 11 月 4 日 King Slide 第8 頁，共 20頁  
產業概況  
AI、ASIC、通用型強勢出擊，2026 年美系 CSP 資本支出估年增 96%  
在 ChatGPT 等 AI 應用陸續推向市場，各大業者紛紛開始研發自有超級大模型，包括 Gemini 
(Google)、Claude (Anthropic)、Llama (Meta) 等，各大品牌開始大舉投入大型語言模型(LLM)市場，
且每一個產品的智慧皆持續提高，部分消費者也陸續從 ChatGPT 陣營轉移至 Gemini 陣營，反映
出消費者開始逐漸提升對 AI 的黏著度。根據 Grand View Research 調查，2024 年全球 AI 市場營
收約 2,792 億美元，預估 2030 年市場規模超過 3 兆美元，擁有超過 30% 的年複合增長率。 
面對這爆發性的長期成長趨勢，CSP 業者(Amazon、Microsoft、Google、Meta)在法說會上一致強
調，AI 投資已轉變為一項橫跨 2026 年的結構性戰略，CSP 大廠不僅持續擴大對 GB300 等次世代
AI 伺服器的採購，更將重點放在資料中心基礎設施的長期戰略規劃，以應對 AI 運算對電力及散熱
系統強勁成長的需求，確保未來數年內擁有足夠的能源與容量來支持持續迭代的大模型部署。本中
心認為，2026 年美國主要 CSP 資本支出將年增 96%，且將有一半的成長來自於資料中心建置，代
表了資料中心的不足及未來伺服器需求。明年不僅僅是 NVIDIA GB300 的天下，AWS、Google、
Meta ASIC 的崛起也將是一大關鍵，其中又以 Google TPU 需求最為強勁，已經有兩個客戶預計將
採購 TPU。 
另外，受惠於 AI 訓練與推論需求爆發的連帶效應，本中心預估 2026 年通用型伺服器出貨量將年增
23%，其中以存儲型伺服器需求最為強勁。隨著生成式 AI 應用普及和使用者數量暴增，CSP 等業
者必須不斷擴充容量，且不僅限於高階 GPU 伺服器，龐大的 AI 模型訓練和推論在使用狀態中將消
耗大量的數據(DATA)，使得存儲型伺服器的需求大增。

### Page 9

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：組裝及零組件產業 2026 年 11 月 4 日 King Slide 第9 頁，共 20頁  
ASIC 進入高速成長期，GPU 需求仍維持擴張 
從 AI 晶片預期出貨來看，GPU 與 ASIC 需求均持續成長，但 2026 年後 ASIC 成長動能明顯加速。
Nvidia GPU 出貨量預估由 2025 年 565.7 萬顆增至 2026 年 650 萬顆、2027 年 850 萬顆，年增
15%及 31%；AMD 亦由 2025 年 90 萬顆增至 2027 年 180 萬顆，顯示通用 GPU 算力需求仍維持
成長。 
相較之下，ASIC 受惠 CSP 加速自研晶片及導入規模擴大，總出貨量預估由 2025 年 380 萬顆大幅
增至 2026 年 725 萬顆、2027 年 1,360 萬顆，年增率達 91%及 88%，成長速度明顯高於 GPU。其
中 Google 仍為最大 ASIC 使用者， 2027 年用量預估達 800 萬顆，AWS 亦增至 320 萬顆，
Microsoft 及 Meta 同步擴大部署。我們認為，在資料中心電力逐漸成為算力擴張瓶頸下， CSP 將
更重視每瓦運算效能及整體運算成本， ASIC 針對特定運算工作的效率優勢有望推升滲透率，惟
GPU 憑藉通用性及軟體生態系優勢，仍將維持重要地位。

獲利調整與股票評價
維持川湖買進評等，目標價 15,000 元
考量客戶記憶體缺料、新舊平台轉換及新創客戶一次性建置完成，本次下修 2026／2027 年營收預
估 12.3%／13.7%至 471.78／730.53 億元，年增 169.6%／54.8%；毛利率分別下修 0.4／0.4 個
百分點至 85.9%／86.5%，EPS 下修 12.4%／14.0%至 323.39／500.57 元，年增 213.3%／54.8%。
本中心看好川湖機構設計、模具開發及自動化製造整合能力，1H27 VR 量產及周邊 Side Rack 導
入帶動高階導軌需求，二廠 2H27 投產增加供應能力，三、五期則支應中長期成長。維持 2027 年
30 倍本益比，以 EPS 500.57 元推得目標價 15,000 元，維持買進評等。

公司簡介  
川湖為 AI 發展浪潮下之主要伺服器導軌受惠廠商  
川湖科技成立於 1986 年，2000-2001 年進入伺服器市場，目前伺服器、網路通訊、雲端資料中心及其週邊設備產品的比重
最高，其次是廚具相關產品，而鉸鏈、滑軌產品主要用於傢俱、鋼櫃、工具櫃、木製傢俱等產品。川湖員工數約 1,500 人，
廠房位於高雄市路竹區(川湖總部)及高雄科學園區(川益科技)。2026 年第二季營收比重中，伺服器業務佔營收約 94%、廚
具與其他業務則約為 6%。

ESG 分析  
分別藉由「在 ESG 議題上的曝險」和「個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力」兩個面向來衡量公司於 ESG 上的表現。 
► ESG 總分： 川湖整體的 ESG 風險評級屬於中等風險，於 Sustainalytics 資料庫所覆蓋的公司中排名亦排行於略為領先
的位置，在電子零組件行業的公司中排名略微領先同業。 
► 在 ESG 議題上的曝險： 川湖的整體曝險屬於中等水準，略為落後電子零組件行業的平均水準。公司較需要改善的  ESG 
議題包含營運方面的碳排、公司在生產中使用原材料的效率(不包括能源和石油產品)、人力資源等。 
► 個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力：川湖在針對重大 ESG 議題的管理水準和執行力屬上等。公司未曾涉入重大財務事件。

附錄：重要揭露事項 
分析師聲明 
主要負責撰寫本研究報告全文或部分內容之分析師，茲針對本報告所載證券或證券發行機構，於此聲明： (1) 文中所述觀點皆準確反映其個人對各證券或
證券發行機構之看法；(2) 研究部分析師於本研究報告中所提出之特定投資建議或觀點，與其過去、現在、未來薪酬的任何部份皆無直接或間接關聯。

投資評等說明 
買進：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持正面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中增持該股。 
持有-超越同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力高於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
持有-落後同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力低於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
賣出：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持負面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中減持該股。 
評估中：本中心之預估、評等、目標價尚在評估中，但仍積極追蹤該個股。 
限制評等：為遵循相關法令規章及/或元大之政策，暫不給予評等及目標價。

### Page 1

Auras Technology (3324.TWO)
3Q26 Preview: Deferrals Weigh on 3Q; ASIC Ramp Underpins 
4Q26E/2027E Growth
CITI'S TAKE 
Auras is scheduled to report 3Q26E results in mid-Nov, followed by its 
annual investor meeting in the following weeks. 3Q26 revenue fell short of 
mgmt. ’s guidance of at least +15% QoQ on GB300 demand deferrals and 
Thailand flooding. We expect GPM below our prior forecast on lower scale 
and front-loaded expenses, and forecast 3Q26 net profit of NT$1.24bn, 13% 
below cons. We now model 4Q26E revenue growth of +20% QoQ on 
deferred demand and the ASIC liquid cooling ramp from Nov, and 2027E 
revenue growth of +50% YoY as multiple ASIC projects broaden the growth 
base. We raise our TP to NT$2,000 (20x 2027E EPS). Reiterate Buy.
3Q26 preview — Auras reported 3Q26 revenue of NT$9.8bn (+13% QoQ/+64% YoY), 
below mgmt. ’s earlier guidance. The shortfall mainly reflected customers deferring 
GB300 demand into 4Q26 and the flooding in Thailand. Given lower revenue scale 
and certain expenses recognized ahead of the upcoming ramp, we expect 3Q26E 
GPM to come in at 28.2% (-0.2 ppt QoQ/-1.6 ppts YoY), below our prior forecast of 
30.6%. With the opex ratio largely in line, we forecast 3Q26 net profit of NT$1.24bn 
(+28% QoQ/+27% YoY), and EPS of NT$13.32, 13% below cons.
Multiple ASIC projects to support 4Q26E/2027E — Mgmt. guided for 4Q26E 
revenue growth of +20% QoQ, driven by deferred 3Q26 revenue and the mass 
production of the ASIC project’s liquid cooling version, which we expect from Nov. 
We raise our 4Q26E growth forecast to +20% QoQ (from +16%), with GPM improving 
to 31.1%. While VR cold plate qualification is ongoing, we believe Auras is well 
positioned to win multiple ASIC projects, with a second project likely to contribute 
from 2H27. We now forecast 2027E revenue growth of +45% YoY, with GPM 
expanding to 32.2% on a rising share of customized projects and a better mix.
Implications — We lower our 2026E earnings forecast by 5% on the softer 3Q26. We 
also raise our 2027E/28E earnings forecasts by 13%/20% on higher ASIC liquid 
cooling shipment assumptions and margin expansion. Our revised TP of NT$2,000 
(from NT$1,800) remains based on 20x 2027E EPS. We believe recent concerns over 
the timing of various AI server projects are largely priced in. Given Auras’ structural 
growth in the server business, widening liquid cooling content across GPU and ASIC 
platforms, and an optimizing global manufacturing footprint, we view current 
valuation as attractive and reiterate our Buy rating.

Bull/Bear: Auras Technology (3324.TWO)

### Page 6

Auras Technology (3324.TWO)
02 November 2026 Citi Research
6
Auras Technology
Company description
Auras Technology was founded in 1998 and focused on the design and
manufacturing of thermal modules for NB/PCs. It quickly became one of
Taiwan’s top two NB cooling component suppliers. Early products included
heatsinks, fins, heat pipes, and fans for NBs, PCs, and smartphones. As
device form factor evolved, the company invested in new technologies such

as ultra-thin vapor chamber in the 2000s to address the thermal challenges
of increasingly compact consumer electronics. During the 2010s, Auras
expanded into graphic card and server cooling and was an early adopter of
liquid cooling technology. By 2012, the company had begun developing liquid
cooling component and was among the first Taiwanese firms to venture into
AI server liquid cooling.
Investment strategy
We rate Auras a Buy. Auras is emerging as a key liquid-cooling supplier as

hyperscalers expand deployment. The company has secured qualifications for
cold plates and manifolds, opening access to a wider slate of Nvidia and CSP
projects. Thailand-based production offers non-China capacity at a scale
aligned with hyperscaler sourcing plans, allowing Auras to compete more
effectively for next-generation products.
Valuation
We value Auras using a PE-based approach, reflecting its accelerating
earnings trajectory and rising share in liquid cooling market of AI servers. Our

TP of NT$2,000 is based on 20x 2027E EPS, which we believe is justified
given the company’s expanding server revenue base, deeper engagement
with CSP customers, and broader product portfolio across cold plates, CDMs,
and CDUs.
Risks
This stock is High Risk based upon our quantitative model, but assigning a
High Risk rating is not supported in our view by other qualitative factors such
as rapid RVL progress and Thailand expansion, and so a High Risk rating has

not been applied. Key downside risks to our investment thesis and target
price include: 1) slower adoption of liquid cooling; 2) heightened price
competition; 3) lower-than-expected share in liquid cooling components;
and 4) emerging alternative cooling technologies.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

M
Update
Hon Hai Precision  | Asia Pacific
Hon Hai Technology Day 2026
(HHTD 2026)
DSX POD accelerates AI infrastructure deployment: Unveiled at GTC Berlin and
displayed at HHTD26, NVIDIA’s DSX POD packages 7x VR200 NVL72 racks, 3x
Spectrum-6 SPX racks, up to ~25 exaFLOPS of NVFP4 performance, and over 10M
components per container ( Exhibit 1 ). Hon Hai said factory integration can shorten
deployment at prepared sites from six months to as little as 14 days, with initial

deliveries expected in early 2027.
Rack capacity and automation support Rubin ramp: HH has doubled AI rack
assembly capacity over the past year to ~2K racks/week. It said its cableless Vera
Rubin compute-tray design cuts assembly time from over two hours to ~20 minutes,
with a further target of five minutes. Greater automation should improve
throughput and consistency while reducing labor intensity, according to HH
management, supporting the Rubin ramp.

Three new EV models expand the CDMS portfolio: HH unveiled a MODEL A+, a
premium B-segment SUV; MODEL K+, an affordable lifestyle SUV for younger
buyers; and MODEL K, a compact truck targeting Japan’s kei segment and last-mile
logistics. The launches expand its EV reference portfolio to 12, broadening its
contract design and manufacturing service  offering across passenger and
commercial vehicles.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Near-term weakness already in the price; maintain Buy and lower TP to NT$286
- Despite near-term margin pressure from softer-than-expected quarterly revenue
and rising depreciation cost, electricity and OPEX, management sees GM
expansion into next year supported by stronger utilization, operating leverage, and
favorable AI-related product mix, in particular substantial upside from wafer

sorting. We lower our earnings projection for 2026/2027 /2028 by 6%/17%/15%
reflecting near-term GM shortfall and potential increasing depreciation cost.
Considering remaining strong AI cycle, we keep our Buy rating but lower our DCF-
derived TP to NT$286 given our lower estimates and concerns on recent
macro/policy uncertainty. Our new TP of NT$286 implies 25x/17x of our 2027 /28
EPS estimates.

### Page 6

King Yuan Electronics Co (2449.TW)
06 November 2026 Citi Research
6
King Yuan Electronics Co
Company description
Founded in 1987, King Yuan Electronics Corp. (KYEC) mainly provides testing
services for the back-end process of semiconductor production globally. With
more than 30 years of industry experience, KYEC has built up firm
relationships with several leading global IC design/IDM customers. Its
headquarters and production facilities are both located in Taiwan, with sales

offices in North America, Japan, and Singapore.
Investment strategy
We rate KYEC as Buy. As a key testing service provider for AI GPU and ASIC
products, KYEC is well positioned to benefit from the significant AI growth
opportunity in the years to come. Currently, KYEC is the major final testing
service provider for its major customer's AI GPU, and we expect KYEC to
continue to secure its dominant share in its customer's upcoming AI GPU
product lines due to its specialty in burn-in testing, smooth product delivery

capability, and its strong business partnership with its customer. We are also
positive over the growth outlook of its smartphone business thanks to the
integration of AI functionality, leading to a better replacement cycle. We
believe KYEC's major customer in smartphone will benefit from an improved
product mix, which will result in longer testing times for the flagship SoC.
Valuation
Our target price for KYEC of NT$286 is derived using a DCF methodology.

Using Bloomberg data, we apply a risk-free rate of 4%, a risk premium of 5%,
and a beta of 1.4x for a 10.8% cost of equity. With 20% corporate tax and a
3.0% interest rate on its debt, our WACC for KYEC is 9.7%. We assume
terminal sales growth of 5%. We model its adjusted EBIT margin to gradually
improve from 23% in 2024 to c30% in 2035E. At our target price, the shares
would trade at 2027 /28E P/B of 4x/4x and 2027 /28E P/E of 25x/17x, in line
with its global peers.

Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent KYEC shares from reaching our target
price include: 1) weaker-than-expected end-demand for global
smartphone/consumer electronic products; 2) slower ramp for AI-related
products; 3) slower-than-expected demand recovery in the automotive and
base station sector; and 4) pricing pressure on increasing competition.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in

## Document 295: [207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 외형 성장 지속, 수익성 개선은 시간이 필요삼성바이오로직스(207940) 외형 성장 지속, 수익성 개선은 시_dcb09b7c

Source PDF: all report pdf/[207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 외형 성장 지속, 수익성 개선은 시간이 필요삼성바이오로직스(207940) 외형 성장 지속, 수익성 개선은 시_dcb09b7c.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-22
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

www.imeritz.com

삼성바이오로직스 (207940)

Company Brief 
제약/바이오 
Analyst 김준영 
junyoung.kim@meritz.co.kr 
RA 김현비 
hyunbee.kim@meritz.co.kr 외형 성장 지속, 수익성 개선은 시간이 필요

3Q26 Review  
3Q26 연결 기준 매출액 1조 4,903억원(YoY +18.5%, QoQ +12.8%), 영업이익 
6,401억원(YoY +1.0%, QoQ +9.2%)으로 컨센서스를 각각 9.8%, 4.7% 상회했다. 
기존 1 ~ 4공장 풀가동 및 미국 Rockville 공장 매출 약 1,000억원과 5공장 PPQ 
물량 인식이 더해지며 외형 성장을 견인했다. 반면 신규 생산시설의 초기 비용 및 제
품 믹스 영향으로 OPM은 42.9%를 기록하며 전년 동기 대비 7.5%p 하락했다. 동
사는 연간 매출 성장률 가이던스를 기존 +15~20%에서 +20%로 상향했다. 당사는 
4분기 Rockville 공장 매출 기여와 기존 공장의 높은 가동률을 감안하여 연간 매출
액 5조 6,135억원(YoY +23.2%)을 예상한다.

신규 생산거점의 외형 확대, 이익 기여는 좀 더 긴 호흡으로 
5공장은 2Q26부터 PPQ 생산을 본격화했으나 상업 생산에 따른 실질적인 이익 기
여는 2027년 하반기에 확대될 전망이다. Rockville 공장은 3Q와 4Q 각각 약 
1,000억원의 매출이 예상되며 2027년 분기별 매출도 유사한 수준을 유지할 것으로 
전망한다. 다만 영업이익 기여까지는 시간이 더 소요될 것으로 예상되어 2027년 이
익 성장은 미국 공장보다 5공장 Ramp-up이 주도할 전망이다.

투자의견 Buy, 적정주가 200만원 유지 
삼성바이오로직스에 대해 투자의견 Buy, 적정주가 200만원을 유지한다. 고객사 수주 
문의가 회복되는 가운데 대형 계약 협의가 진행 중이며 6공장 역시 연말 또는 내년 
초 착공을 목표로 하고 있다. 다만 신규 수주가 실제 계약으로 이어지는 시점과 5공
장의 상업 생산 전환 속도가 중기 실적의 핵심 변수로 판단한다. 추가 대형 수주 확
보 시 5공장 이후의 성장 가시성도 높아질 것으로 예상한다. 
PolyPeptide 인수는 펩타이드 CDMO로 사업을 확장한다는 점에서 긍정적이다. 인
수 완료 이후 다음 분기부터 연결 매출 인식이 가능하나 유의미한 영업이익 기여까지
는 최소 2년 이상 소요될 전망이다. PolyPeptide 인수는 현재 추정치에서 반영하지 
않았으며 향후 인수 완료 시 연결 OPM 희석 가능성을 감안할 필요가 있다. 한편 최
근 법원 항고심에서 배양부터 정제까지의 생산공정이 쟁의행위 기간에도 정상 수행되
어야 할 보전작업으로 인정되면서 파업에 따른 생산 차질 우려는 완화될 것으로 판단
한다. 노사 협상은 여전히 진행 중이나 생산 안정성에 대한 고객사 우려가 낮아질 경
우 신규 수주에도 긍정적으로 작용할 전망이다. 
 Meritz Research  2026. 10. 22

Buy (20거래일 평균종가 대비 상승 여력 기준) 
적정주가 (12개월) 2,000,000원

## Document 326: Anthropic路演最快本周二启动，AI抛售潮后IPO定价迎大考

Source PDF: all report pdf/Anthropic路演最快本周二启动，AI抛售潮后IPO定价迎大考.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

若路演进展顺利， Anthropic 有望在 11 ⽉ 16 ⽇当周完成定价并登陆纳斯达克，赶在 11 ⽉美
国感恩节假期前完成上市。上周五报道称， AI 股⼤跌后，部分潜在投资者讨论的 Anthropic
估值已降⾄约 1.2 万亿⾄ 1.3 万亿美元，甚⾄有⼈考虑 1.1 万亿，远低于市场早前讨论的约 2
万亿。
摘要：
尽管 AI 板块近期遭遇剧烈抛售， Anthropic 仍决定顶住市场压⼒推进 IPO ，此次⼤规模上市将直
接测试公开市场对前沿 AI 资产的⻛险偏好与定价意愿。
据《华尔街⽇报》最新报道，⼀周前，投资者预期 Anthropic 的估值⽬标约为 1.5 万亿美元，但
在经历了 AI 公司股票的暴跌后，⼀些主要投资者现在希望⽬标估值更低。⽬前，该公司⾼管⼀
直在与⾼盛和摩根⼠丹利的银⾏家会⾯，商讨估值区间的最终确定。
据彭博 6 ⽇援引⼀位知情⼈⼠透露，部分潜在投资者讨论的估值已降⾄约 1.2 万亿⾄ 1.3 万亿美
元，甚⾄有⼈考虑低⾄ 1.1 万亿美元。
报道称， Anthropic 本周已安排与关键投资者会⾯，⽬标是让其股票在下周四或周五开始交易。

该公司仍有可能推迟估值⽬标并等待更有利的市场条件，但这可能会引发市场恐慌。
此次 IPO 的进展与最终定价不仅关乎 Anthropic ⾃⾝的融资规模，更将成为整个 AI 产业链估值的
⻛向标，直接影响市场对 AI 基础设施⾼昂资本开⽀及其商业化前景的重新评估。
冲刺感恩节前上市，估值预期⾯临下调
Anthropic 此次 IPO 的时间表已进⼊关键阶段。
据彭博社报道，根据相关规定，在 10 ⽉ 26 ⽇公开提交招股书后， Anthropic 最快可于 11 ⽉ 10 ⽇开
始正式 IPO 营销。若进展顺利，公司有望在 11 ⽉ 16 ⽇当周完成定价，赶在 11 ⽉ 26 ⽇美国感恩节
假期前登陆纳斯达克，股票代码为 “ANT” 。
然⽽，市场的剧烈波动使得估值成为各⽅博弈的核⼼。数⽉前，市场曾讨论过约 2 万亿美元的估
值数字，但 Anthropic 现在⼏乎肯定需要接受⼀个低得多的估值。据彭博 6 ⽇援引⼀位知情⼈⼠
透露，部分潜在投资者讨论的估值已降⾄约 1.2 万亿⾄ 1.3 万亿美元，甚⾄有⼈考虑低⾄ 1.1 万亿

美元。
对于投⾏⽽⾔，定价策略⾄关重要。如果⽬标设定过⾼，投资者需求可能受损并导致股价下
跌；若区间过低，公司和银⾏家将⾯临 “ 把钱留在桌⾯上 ” 的批评。在实际操作中，银⾏家有时会
⿎励公司提出略低于预期的价格区间，以便随后以投资者需求强劲为由上调价格。预计本周提
交的⽂件将详细说明计划出售的股票数量及价格，并可能披露已锁定按 IPO 价格认购的知名投资
者。
政治与市场双重阻⼒， AI 概念⾯临重估
Anthropic 此次 IPO ⾯临的挑战不仅是投资者对其⾃⾝估值的分歧，更受到整个 AI 产业链⻛险偏
好变化的影响。美国中期选举后，⼀批对 AI 持怀疑态度的⼈⼠掌权，并出台了可能减缓美国数据
中⼼发展的暂停令，导致纳斯达克综合指数成分股在⼀⽇内蒸发了 2.5 万亿美元市值。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
60 收藏 分享到 :
市场波动曾推动芝加哥期权交易所的波动率指数（ VIX ）升破 25 ⾄ 28 上⽅。 Renaissance
Capital ⾼级策略师 Matt Kennedy 表⽰，当 VIX 处于 25 以上⽔平时，企业通常会考虑推迟 IPO 。此
外，投资者对巨额 AI 投资及其潜在回报缺乏清晰度的担忧⽇益增加，此前 Anthropic 的竞争对⼿
OpenAI 在最新⼀轮融资中筹集的资⾦不到最初⽬标的⼀半，估值也低于⽬标的 1.25 万亿美元。
不过，市场随后出现了⼀定程度的修复。标普 500 指数和费城半导体指数的反弹，以及 VIX 指数
的回落，可能为 Anthropic 继续推进上市提供了额外的信⼼。 Matt Kennedy 认为， Anthropic 的
IPO 不同于⼀般企业上市，当前市场环境未必⾜以迫使其推迟上市，但投资者对折价和估值的要

求可能更⾼。
业绩⾼增⻓遭遇放缓，投资者紧盯算⼒成本
估值争议的另⼀核⼼，是 Anthropic 能否证明⾃⾝的营收增⻓⾜以⽀撑庞⼤的上市估值。招股书
显⽰， Anthropic 第三季度营收达到 169.56 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 1127% 。但增⻓速度已出现放缓迹
象，第三季度环⽐增幅降⾄ 47% ，远低于第⼆季度 144% 的环⽐增速。
利润表现同样承压。第三季度， Anthropic 经营亏损 5.67 亿美元，净亏损达到 232.91 亿美元。业
绩转亏主要受到约 50 亿美元短期算⼒采购成本，以及约 20 亿美元员⼯捐赠匹配计划相关⾮现⾦
费⽤的影响。更令市场关注的是，公司披露的计算能⼒采购及基础设施承诺⽀出总额⾼达 8954
亿美元。
据路透社报道，投资者⽬前仍在等待 Anthropic 的 10 ⽉份营收数据，希望借此判断公司能否扭转
近期增⻓放缓的趋势。营收表现、企业客⼾使⽤量以及算⼒成本控制能⼒，将是路演期间投资
者重点追问的核⼼指标。
融资规模或创纪录，⾏业⻛向标意义凸显

作为⾸批以开发前沿 AI 模型为核⼼业务并尝试登陆公开市场的公司之⼀， Anthropic 此次发⾏的
规模和定价将为整个 AI ⾏业提供重要参照。
市场正密切关注 Anthropic 的 IPO 能否与 Elon Musk 旗下 SpaceX 创纪录的上市表现相媲美。
SpaceX 在此前的发⾏中估值约为 1.77 万亿美元，筹集资⾦规模在 750 亿美元⾄ 862 亿美元之间。
⽽据路透社报道， Anthropic 此次发⾏规模甚⾄可能达到 1000 亿美元。
由⾼盛和摩根⼠丹利牵头，另有 20 家银⾏参与的这笔超⼤型 IPO ，其实际认购情况将给出明确的
信号：在经历 AI 概念股⼤幅调整后，公开市场究竟愿意为前沿 AI 公司的⻓期增⻓潜⼒⽀付多⾼的
溢价。

### Page 2

forecasts are based are reasonable, the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially
from these forecasts.
2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
The Cotality home value index, covering all of Australia, fell
1.2% in October with slight revisions to previous months
again shaving a further 0.3% off prices. The correction
has now seen prices decline 6.7% from their March peak

with annual growth firmly in negative at –2.8%yr. Turnover
continues fall as well, down 22.5%yr. Housing-related
tax policy changes, interest rate rises and heightened
uncertainty have triggered a sharp contraction in markets.
The downturn is now comparable to the previous two
housing market corrections. Nationally, the downturn in
2022-23 saw prices decline 7.5% and turnover fall 29% peak
to trough. The 2017-18 correction saw prices fall 8.6% and

turnover by 24%, with larger falls in Sydney and Melbourne.
Note that unless specified, figures in titles, tables and
commentary are in non‑seasonally adjusted terms, consistent
with the ‘headline’ figures reported by Cotality. However,
figures shown in charts are in seasonally adjusted terms.
Preliminary estimates of turnover show rolling 3mth volumes
down 5.6%qtr in October, previous estimates again revised
lower. The monthly pattern suggests declines re ‑accelerated

through September‑October after stabilising somewhat in July ‑
August. The RBA's interest rate rise in late September and a
further expected tightening likely contributed to the shift.
The detail shows Brisbane and Perth are now leading the
way in terms of monthly price declines although corrections
to date remain significantly larger in Sydney and Melbourne
while smaller capitals and regional areas continue to see much
milder downturns. Across price tiers, all segments are now

showing similar rates of decline after top price tiers had seen
a more pronounced and earlier downturn. 'Top tier' segments
have tended to lead recent price cycles.
Looking across jurisdictions,
• Sydney prices dropped 1.6% in October having averaged
similar‑sized declines over the previous five months. While
all segments and tiers continue to record falls, the last few
months has seen the pace of decline moderate a touch in the
top tier segments that have led cycles in the past, although

they are still a long way from stabilisation.
• Melbourne prices declined 0.8%mth, in line with
September's move and continuing the slower pace of
retreat. As noted, this likely reflects the weaker starting
point coming into the correction, prices already at 5½ year
lows. Interestingly, the detail shows the moderation in price
declines in recent months has been more pronounced for
'top tier' units where prices are back where they were in
2016.
Housing downturn deepens

### Page 3

forecasts are based are reasonable, the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially
from these forecasts.
3WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
• Brisbane prices are tracking a very fast rate of decline
that is also seeing large downward revisions. Prices fall
2% in October with September and August now both
showing 2.1% falls, revised down from 1.5% and 1.6%

respectively (August having previously been estimated at
just 1%, July's move having seen a similar series of revisions
–1.2% to –1.8%). The combined effect has taken the rolling
3mth change to –6.1%qtr from –4.7%qtr last months and
–2.7%qtr in August and –0.6%qtr in July. At 0.4%yr, annual
price growth has virtually stalled flat having run at 18%yr as
recently as May. All tiers and segments are recording similar
rates of decline.
• Perth is seeing something very similar, prices dropping a

further 1.7% in October, with September's decline revised
down to 2.1% from –1.2% and August's to 2.0% from –1.7%.
At 3.9%yr, annual growth is still positive but off steeply
from the 24% pace back in May. As with Brisbane, all tiers
and segments are recording similar rates of decline.
• Adelaide dwelling prices posted a somewhat milder 1.2%
decline in October, , September's –1.3% decline marked
down to –1.7%. Annual price growth slowed to 3.7%yr,

similar to Perth's but with less of a whiplash from strong
gains to large declines. Turnover has also been much
steadier, sales holding flat over the last three months and
down just 12.4%yr.
• The smaller capitals were again more mixed, price growth
still positive in Darwin (0.6%mth, 9.7%yr), relatively resilient
in Hobart (+0.5%mth, 5.5%yr) but weaker in Canberra
(–0.7%mth, –3.7%yr).
• Regional markets showed a 0.9% decline in Qld but slightly
milder declines in NSW (–0.7%) and Victoria (0.6%). Annual

prices growth is in the 1.4 ‑3.5%yr range.
All up, the October updates make for bleak reading. With the
RBA widely expected to raise interest rates again, the near
term outlook remains very challenging. Next week's consumer
sentiment update will show how this is shaping housing ‑
related sentiment – so far, consumer price expectations have
weakened but stopped short of swinging to the clear negative
consensus that emerged in the 2022 ‑23 correction. That now

looks to be at risk. Either way, momentum looks set to remain
weak into year‑end and, barring a significantly improved
inflation and interest rate outlook, well into 2027.

## Document 354: Central banks_ a pause while waiting for more information (2026-10-29)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Central banks_ a pause while waiting for more information (2026-10-29).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Paris, 30 October 2026

EcoFlash

Central banks: a pause while waiting for more information

As expected, the Fed, the ECB, and the BoJ left their rates unchanged this week. This pause , however, does not mark 
the end of the cycle. The ECB’s tone is becoming more balanced, weighing inflationary risks against tightening financial 
conditions, but one final rate hike in December remains our base case. Across the Atlantic, Kevin Warsh describes an 
environment conducive to further tightening, while remaining cautious about the risk of a credit shock. In Japan, the 
lasting exit from the deflationary cycle is taking shape, and monetary policy adjustment is expected to continue 
gradually. We anticipate two rate hikes by the Fed by January and four by the BoJ by the second half of 2028.

Eurozone: A more balanced tone, but the course remains unchanged. The ECB’s statement marks a shift from September. 
It now explicitly acknowledges both upside risks to inflation (the magnitude and persistence of the energy shock) and downsid e 
pressures (tightening financial conditions). The statement that inflation would  remain “well above target for an extended period” 
no longer appears. In essence, however, there is little change. A rate hike in December remains our base case scenario, widel y 
anticipated and implicitly signaled by Christine Lagarde during the Q&A (“If the current situation is not called into question by the 
next set of economic data, the path is clear”). Lagarde presented the rise in sovereign yields as a global phenomenon that is  
tightening financial conditions; regarding the TPI, she stuck to the usual stance: the ECB will intervene if monetary policy 
transmission were to be hampered “by disorderly movements not justified by fundamentals” . By December, the indirect and 
second-round effects of the energy shock will be closely scrutini sed. From our perspective, September’s producer prices offer 
clearer signals, but the increase remains limited to a few sectors: energy, electricity-intensive industries and electrical equipment, 
driven by IA 1. For now, companies in the Eurozone, particularly in the services sector, appear to be absorbing the shock. The 
European Commission’s survey released this Thursday indicates a rise in the sales price index in October, which remains in line 
with recent months.

United States: The Fed navigates between risks. The FOMC pauses after September’s rate hike and maintains the federal 
funds target at 3.75%-4.0%. However, Fed Chairman Kevin Warsh outlined a landscape conducive to further tightening: stronger 
growth, “rather positive” labor market conditions, and, above all, inflation that is “too high.” As a result, two dissenting votes (out 
of 12) were cast in favor of a 25bps hike.  But the committee also appears to fear the adverse consequences of tightening too 
quickly, which is why it is waiting to “accumulate more information.” AI is increasingly at the heart of monetary policymaking. In 
particular, K. Warsh warned against the risks of an abrupt tightening of interest rate conditions, including for the AI investment 
cycle - which triggered a steepening of the yield curve. He also announced that he would soon (on November 16) attend a hearing 
of the “Productivity and Employment” task force to “refine his views” on the implications of AI for the dual mandate. Governor Lisa 
Cook, who voted for a 25bps hike, had noted ahead of the committee meeting “ongoing inflationary pressures” and a risk that they 
might not “subside quickly”. In addition, K. Warsh announced that there would be fewer meetings resulting in rate decisions: 7 in 
2027 and 6 in 2028, compared with 8 today. The change is expected to take effect starting in the fall of 2027. The meetings freed 
up by this change would allow the committee to focus on “strategic deliberations” and long-term issues.

Japan: A status quo, but a tightening cycle set to continue as the economy emerges from deflation. The Bank of Japan 
decided, in line with our forecasts and the consensus, to keep its policy rate target steady at 1.25% at its October 29-30 meeting. 
One dissenting vote called for a rate hike (25bps), contrasting with the two dovish dissenting votes (opposed to a rate hike) at the 
previous meeting. As in the US, t he decision is justified by the need to further analy se risks to economic activity, prices, and 
financial conditions. Above all, the central bank’s outlook for economic activity and prices marks a turning point: it explicitly states 
that “core inflation has reached a level generally in line with price stability” and that it is “expected to remain there.” Governor Ueda 
refined this by noting that it is now necessary to “anchor” inflation at this level before declaring the target achieved. Furthermore, 
the emphasis on “upside risks to prices” and the fact that an y rise in core inflation is now deemed “undesirable” indicate that 
monetary tightening will continue.

The monetary tightening cycle continues. The next steps are likely to be more significant in the United States, where inflation 
has exceeded the target for nearly six years and coexists with a favorable outlook for growth and employment. In the Eurozone, 
the rise in inflation is primarily due to a supply shock in energy costs (though it is also fueled by demand in the United St ates). It 
continues to have little impact on measures of core inflation, including those tracked by the ECB, such as the H ICP; and 
households’ long-term inflation expectations remain anchored. The degree of monetary tightening also differs. In real terms, the 
ECB’s current rates (the deposit facility rate deflated by the core CPI) hover around 0%, a level that more or less corresponds to 
estimates of the natural rate. The Fed, meanwhile, remains in accommodative territory: the real rate (Fed Funds deflated by Core

1 This is a point underscored by the first PPI releases for September (Spain, Italy).

### Page 2

PCE) is at around 0.8%, 40bps below the committee’s implied long-term level (1.2%). Japan remains an outlier: the rise in rates 
preceded the inflationary shock and, until now, has been more a matter of normalization. It is therefore more extensive and spread 
out over time. The central bank is adjusting to the new inflation regime and remains cautious in its exit from accommodative 
policies. The starting point is significantly lower, with real rates still negative, but the adjustment cycle should ultimately bring it to 
the upper end of the neutral range estimated by the BoJ (1.1% -2.5%). We therefore anticipate one final rate hike by the ECB in 
December, two more hikes by the Fed, first in December and then in January, and four spaced-out hikes by the BoJ through the 
second half of 2028.

Anis Bensaidani   
Economist, United States, Japan 
anis.bensaidani@bnpparibas.com

Guillaume Derrien 
Economist, Europe 
guillaume.a.derrien@bnpparibas.com

## Document 361: COP31_ Why it still matters for businesses

Source PDF: all report pdf/COP31_ Why it still matters for businesses.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Is COP still worth following? We think so. What matters for
businesses is whether this year’s COP31 can improve the
business case for action. Here are four areas to watch

Coco Zhang and Gerben Hieminga
The global climate landscape has changed signiﬁcantly since world
leaders committed 11 years ago to limiting global warming to well
below 2°C and pursuing eﬀorts to limit warming to 1.5°C.
Geopolitical tensions, policy shifts, trade protectionism, rising
energy demand… they have all made it harder for governments to
turn climate commitments into action.
That raises an important question for businesses: Is the Conference
of the Parties (COP) still worth following?
Our answer is yes.
Businesses do not just invest in climate targets; they invest in viable
projects and operations. But many of them still face economic and
practical barriers. Grid constraints and high electricity costs can
slow electriﬁcation. Greener industrial products can struggle to

### Page 2

attract buyers willing to pay a premium. And climate adaptation
plans do not always translate into investable projects.
Businesses cannot solve these challenges alone. In a 2025 UN
survey of more than 2,000 CEOs across 128 countries, 92% said
strong global governance and coordinated policy are “critical” or
“important” to implementing their sustainability agendas.
That is why COP is still relevant. It can help align policy priorities,
improving investment conditions, and connecting businesses with

the right resources. At COP31, four areas will be particularly
important for businesses to watch.
Electriﬁcation marries economic growth
with sustainability
Electriﬁcation is increasingly important for economic growth,
energy security, and sustainability. Demand from AI, EVs, heat
pumps and industrial decarbonisation is accelerating electricity
use, which is growing twice as fast as overall energy demand.
Electricity also punches above its weight economically, accounting

for 23% of global ﬁnal energy consumption but powering 46% of
global GDP. This implies that high value-added sectors rely more on
electricity than the overall economy.
Electricity’s economic contribution
outweighs its share of energy use

Source: ING Research, International Energy Agency
COP31’s push to accelerate electriﬁcation reﬂects this trend. The
COP31 presidency has set a goal of increasing electricity’s share of
ﬁnal energy consumption from 23% today to 35% by 2035.
Getting there will require addressing a major constraint: the grid. As
we have written, grid congestion could slow electriﬁcation, increase
costs, and delay electricity-dependent economic activity.
The key question at COP31 is how governments turn this ambition

into action. We think eﬀective action could include:
Establish a detailed electriﬁcation roadmap with interim
benchmarks to guide progress and sustain momentum across
future COPs
Enhance knowledge-sharing platforms. The International
Smart Grid Action Network has extensive experience sharing
best practices, but could go further by translating knowledge
into direct technical assistance and ready-to-deploy models
Set country- or sector-level targets and pathways to encourage

industry alignment and commitment
Improve access to ﬁnance by expanding dedicated ﬁnancing
for developing countries, while lowering upfront costs in Europe
Improve the economics. Coordinate energy pricing reforms to
make electriﬁcation more competitive with fossil fuel
combustion alternatives, including by reducing taxes and grid
fees that raise electricity prices
Strengthen infrastructure by encouraging governments to
reduce permitting barriers (e.g. Europe and the US), advance

### Page 4

regulatory support for power demand ﬂexibility, and expand
cross-border grids where needed (e.g. Southeast Asia)
If COP31 delivers meaningful action in these areas, the implications
for businesses could be signiﬁcant. Stronger policy could accelerate
near-term grid connections and enhance long-term investment
conﬁdence. Meanwhile, companies may need to work more closely
with utilities and regulators to secure the power they need.
Green industrialisation remains critical

But electriﬁcation is only part of the story. Industry is another
major source of process emissions, with sectors such as steel,
cement, and aviation accounting for roughly 20% of the global
total. Cutting these emissions is particularly diﬃcult because many
industrial processes still have few cost-eﬀective alternatives without
lengthy site overhauls.
COP31 has therefore put green industrialisation high on the
agenda, building on COP30’s progress. Following the Belém

Declaration on Global Green Industrialisation at COP30, a coalition
led by the Brazilian and UK governments mobilised hundreds of
millions of dollars in concessional ﬁnance for Brazilian industries
and awarded funding to 12 hydrogen projects. Over time, these
eﬀorts could reshape manufacturing processes and investment.
But many industrial decarbonisation technologies still face
signiﬁcant barriers to scaling. Their high cost premiums mean
economics depend heavily on government support. Demand for

cleaner industrial products remains limited. And the necessary
infrastructure for many is still underdeveloped.
Hard-to-abate sectors’ green premium is
way higher than what companies are willing
to pay

COP31 is not setting a new level of ambition for green
industrialisation. It’s focusing on implementing the existing
agenda. One priority is narrowing the demand gap. COP31 can
coordinate governments to create more demand for low-carbon
industrial products through long-term public procurement. If such
eﬀorts gain momentum at COP31, they could provide the long-run
revenue certainty investors need to back green industrial projects at

scale.
Climate ﬁnance faces multiple gaps
To realise the targets and priorities set forth at COP, investment is
crucial. And total ﬂows are growing. Global climate ﬁnance reached
around $2tn in 2024 and continued to grow in 2025, although at a
slower rate.
Global climate ﬁnance continues to grow

Source: Climate Policy Initiative, ING Research
But the headline number masks signiﬁcant gaps. First, even though
climate mitigation (i.e. emissions reduction) ﬁnance accounted for
$1.9tn of the $2tn total, the level falls well short of estimated
needs, which range from $7.6tn-$9.6tn through 2050. In areas such
as electriﬁcation and green industrialisation, funding is needed not
only for technologies, but also for the infrastructure and demand

needed to scale them.
Second, only $64bn of the total went exclusively to adaptation, an
area requiring surging investment because of intensifying climate
risks. Third, a substantial share of climate ﬁnance stays within
advanced economies or comes from domestic sources.
International ﬂows to help developing countries totalled only
$175bn, far below COP29’s target of $1.3tn per year by 2035.

Source: ING Research, Climate Policy Initiative, UN, UNEP, Natural
Resources Defense Council
More climate products and solutions are emerging in the latter two
segments. But their higher risks or weaker business cases can make
it diﬃcult to attract capital without supportive policies. Investors are
actively turning to blended ﬁnance, which uses public capital to
help de-risk projects and mobilise private investment. Yet many still
lack the data, resources and connections needed to move projects

forward. This is where COP31 can help. As we discuss in the next
section, it can help governments create more favourable
investment conditions and connect suppliers, buyers and investors.
Adaptation is becoming a business
imperative and opportunity
Business awareness of climate adaptation has grown, as many
businesses are experiencing more disruptions ﬁrsthand. Our
previous analysis also shows how drought on Europe’s Rhine River,
hurricanes in the US, and other extreme weather events are already

aﬀecting supply chains across sectors. In aggregate, physical
climate risks could cost companies in the S&P Global 1200 index
$1.2tn annually by 2050.
One barrier to corporate investment in climate adaptation is that it
is still seen primarily as a way to avoid losses. But adaptation can
also create business value. Investments in resilient assets, cooling
systems, supplier diversiﬁcation and climate-resilient materials can
improve reliability, competitiveness and long-term growth.

Research from the World Resources Institute suggests that every $1
invested in adaptation can generate up to $10 in beneﬁts. That
return could be even higher for highly climate-exposed sectors. For
Cookie settings
At ING we respect your right to choose how your data is used.

please conﬁrm your preferences. You can change your cookie

Performance Cookies
Targeting Cookies

### Page 8

example, the World Economic Forum estimates returns of $19–$43
per dollar invested in the food and beverage sector.
COP can help turn adaptation priorities into investable projects.
Since the Paris Agreement, countries have developed National
Adaptation Plans (NAPs), but many remain high-level and lack
speciﬁc, actionable projects. Recent COP eﬀorts have sought to
change this by encouraging governments to translate their NAPs
into portfolios of investable adaptation projects.

But identifying projects does not guarantee ﬁnancing. This is where
COP31 could make a diﬀerence. Its proposed Climate
Implementation Bridge (BRIDGE) initiative could make projects
easier for investors to identify and assess by centralising
information on technical requirements, risks, costs and beneﬁts.
Expanding the initiative to more countries could give more banks,
investors and companies access to potential adaptation
investments.
For businesses, a successful COP31 could therefore help unlock

capital for adaptation projects. The beneﬁts could extend
throughout supply chains, as companies increasingly depend on
the climate resilience of their suppliers to maintain reliable
operations.
Conclusion
The most eﬀective corporate sustainability strategies support long-
term growth, but they are more eﬀective with international
coordination and strong policy support. COP can bring
governments and businesses together to provide such a
framework, even amid geopolitical tensions and concerns over

slowing progress. COP31 may not deliver a landmark global
agreement, but practical advances in these four priorities could still
have immediate relevance for businesses.

## Document 402: India_ Degrees of rupee defence (2026-10-16)

### Page 1

TAKING STOCK

The Reserve Bank of India (RBI) has adopted a 
multi-layered defence strategy to support the 
rupee this year.

This has entailed a combination of outright 
intervention in spot/ forwards, regulatory 
action, liquidity steps, administrative measures 
and moves to curb import -related dollar 
demand.

The rupee has appreciated by 1.8% against the 
dollar in the past five days since recent 
measures took effect, paring YTD depreciation 
to 5.5% . High oil prices, weakness in capital 
inflows, a firm dollar , tariffs/ geopolitical 
worries, energy , and risk -off sentiments have 
kept the INR under pressure since last year.
